    //@  assigns \nothing;
    //@ also
    //@  requires arr != null;

        }

public class IsPrefix {
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
        }

        return true;
    }
}

```

public class IsPrefix {
    //@  requires pat != null;
    //@  requires txt != null;
    //@  requires pat.length() <= txt.length();
    //@  assigns \nothing;
    //@  ensures \result == (\forall int i; 0 <= i < pat.length(); pat.charAt(i) == txt.charAt(i));
    //@ also
    //@  requires pat != null;
    //@  requires txt != null;
    //@  requires pat.length() > txt.length();
    //@  assigns \nothing;
    //@  ensures \result == false;
    //@ behaviors disjoint;
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= pat.length();
        //@ maintaining \forall int j; 0 <= j < i; pat.charAt(j) == txt.charAt(j);
        //@ loop_writes i;
        //@ decreases pat.length() - i;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
        }

        return true;
    }
}

public class Find {
    //@  requires arr != null;
    //@  requires \exists int i; 0 <= i < arr.length; arr[i] == elem;
    //@  assigns \nothing;
    //@  ensures 0 <= \result < arr.length;
    //@  ensures \forall int i; 0 <= i < \result; arr[i] != elem;
    //@  ensures arr[\result] == elem;
    //@ also
    //@  requires arr == null || \forall int i; 0 <= i < arr.length; arr[i] != elem;
    //@  assigns \nothing;
    //@  ensures \result == -1;
    //@ behaviors disjoint;
    //@ behaviors complete;
    //@ pure

            return -1;
        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= arr.length;
        //@ maintaining \forall int j; 0 <= j < i; arr[j] != elem;
        //@ loop_writes i;
        //@ decreases arr.length - i;

        return -1;

public class ScalarProductSaturated {
    public static int[] scalarProductSaturated(int[] vec, int a) {
        if (vec == null) return null;
        int ret[] = new int[vec.length];
        for (int i = 0; i < vec.length; i++) {
            long prod = (long) vec[i] * (long) a;
            if (prod > Integer.MAX_VALUE) {
                ret[i] = Integer.MAX_VALUE;
            } else if (prod < Integer.MIN_VALUE) {
                ret[i] = Integer.MIN_VALUE;
            } else {
                ret[i] = (int) prod;
            }
        }
        return ret;
    }
}

```

public class ScalarProductSaturated {
    //@ requires vec != null;
    //@ assigns \fresh(ret) && ret != null && ret.length == vec.length;
    //@ ensures \result != null && \result.length == vec.length;
    //@ ensures (\forall int i; 0 <= i < vec.length; 
    //@            (\let long prod = (long)vec[i] * (long)a;
    //@             prod > Integer.MAX_VALUE ==> \result[i] == Integer.MAX_VALUE &&
    //@             prod < Integer.MIN_VALUE ==> \result[i] == Integer.MIN_VALUE &&
    //@             Integer.MIN_VALUE <= prod && prod <= Integer.MAX_VALUE ==> \result[i] == (int)prod));
    public static int[] scalarProductSaturated(int[] vec, int a) {
        if (vec == null) return null;
        int ret[] = new int[vec.length];
        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= vec.length;
        //@ maintaining \forall int j; 0 <= j < i;
        //@   (\let long prod = (long)vec[j] * (long)a;
        //@    prod > Integer.MAX_VALUE ==> ret[j] == Integer.MAX_VALUE &&
        //@    prod < Integer.MIN_VALUE ==> ret[j] == Integer.MIN_VALUE &&
        //@    Integer.MIN_VALUE <= prod && prod <= Integer.MAX_VALUE ==> ret[j] == (int)prod);
        //@ loop_writes ret[*], i;
        //@ decreases vec.length - i;

            if (prod > Integer.MAX_VALUE) {
                ret[i] = Integer.MAX_VALUE;
            } else if (prod < Integer.MIN_VALUE) {
                ret[i] = Integer.MIN_VALUE;
            } else {

    //@  ensures \result == false;
    //@ behaviors disjoint;
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= pat.length();
        //@ maintaining \forall int j; 0 <= j < i; pat.charAt(j) == txt.charAt(j);
        //@ loop_writes i;
        //@ decreases pat.length() - i;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
        }

        return true;
    }

public class IsPrefix {
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
        }

        return true;
    }
}

public class IsPrefix {
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
        }

        return true;
    }

public class IsPrefix {
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
        }

        return true;
    }
}

public class ScalarProductSaturated {
    public static int[] scalarProductSaturated(int[] vec, int a) {
        if (vec == null) return null;
        int ret[] = new int[vec.length];
        for (int i = 0; i < vec.length; i++) {
            long prod = (long) vec[i] * (long) a;
            if (prod > Integer.MAX_VALUE) {
                ret[i] = Integer.MAX_VALUE;
            } else if (prod < Integer.MIN_VALUE) {
                ret[i] = Integer.MIN_VALUE;
            } else {
                ret[i] = (int) prod;
            }
        }
        return ret;
    }
}

public class IsPrefix {
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
        }

        return true;
    }

public class IsPrefix {
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
        }

        return true;
    }
}

    public static int[] scalarProductSaturated(int[] vec, int a) {
        if (vec == null) return null;
        int ret[] = new int[vec.length];
        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= vec.length;
        //@ maintaining \forall int j; 0 <= j < i;
        //@   (\let long prod = (long)vec[j] * (long)a;
        //@    prod > Integer.MAX_VALUE ==> ret[j] == Integer.MAX_VALUE &&
        //@    prod < Integer.MIN_VALUE ==> ret[j] == Integer.MIN_VALUE &&
        //@    Integer.MIN_VALUE <= prod && prod <= Integer.MAX_VALUE ==> ret[j] == (int)prod);
        //@ loop_writes ret[*], i;
        //@ decreases vec.length - i;
        for (int i = 0; i < vec.length; i++) {
            long prod = (long) vec[i] * (long) a;
            if (prod > Integer.MAX_VALUE) {
                ret[i] = Integer.MAX_VALUE;
            } else if (prod < Integer.MIN_VALUE) {
                ret[i] = Integer.MIN_VALUE;
            } else {
                ret[i] = (int) prod;
            }
        }
        return ret;
    }

public class ScalarProductSaturated {
    public static int[] scalarProductSaturated(int[] vec, int a) {
        if (vec == null) return null;
        int ret[] = new int[vec.length];
        for (int i = 0; i < vec.length; i++) {
            long prod = (long) vec[i] * (long) a;
            if (prod > Integer.MAX_VALUE) {
                ret[i] = Integer.MAX_VALUE;
            } else if (prod < Integer.MIN_VALUE) {
                ret[i] = Integer.MIN_VALUE;
            } else {
                ret[i] = (int) prod;
            }
        }
        return ret;
    }
}

public class ScalarProductSaturated {

      @*/

        if (vec == null) return null;

        /*@ 
          @ maintaining 0 <= i <= vec.length;
          @ maintaining (\forall int j; 0 <= j < i;

            if (prod > Integer.MAX_VALUE) {

    public static int[] scalarProductSaturated(int[] vec, int a) {
        if (vec == null) return null;
        int ret[] = new int[vec.length];
        for (int i = 0; i < vec.length; i++) {
